Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the oscillations structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 699. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 934 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).
